# Defensibility report — `wiki/products/shellfish.md`

This report joins every numerical claim on the page to its citation chain and the audit metadata recorded in `data/evidence/wiki_edit_audit.jsonl`. It is generated by `node tools/build-defensibility-report.mjs <page>` and is byte-identical given the same inputs.

## How to read this

- Sections follow the page's own H2 structure.
- Each claim shows the claim value, the section it appeared in, the cited sources in that section, and — when the cited source page itself has audit rows — the retrieval date, models used, agreement state, and span id from each row.
- Claims without an in-section citation chain (no `[[sources/...]]`, `[[regulations/...]]`, or `[[testing/...]]` link in the same H2 section as the claim) are listed at the end as uncited claims. A page-level sources block alone is not credited here — it would inherit every claim onto every listed source and defeat the report's purpose.
- This v1 verifies that a citation exists. It does not yet verify that the cited source contains the claimed value — that is the v2 PDF-side check.
